About these results

Prop calls, season546-55750% right · quarterbacks 70-88, running backs 173-200, receivers 303-269
Touchdown chances, season23%Leo’s average chance · 24% scored · 1,732 players
How Leo is graded
  • Every number is frozen before kickoff and never changed after. Only that frozen read is graded.
  • Three calls per game from November 2: moneyline, spread and total. Each is a call or no call, and only calls are graded. Units: the moneyline at the price when Leo’s read froze, the spread and total at -110, one unit per call.
  • Moneyline: a team Leo gives more than 60%, priced better than -230. A dearer favorite gets no call.
  • Spread: the side Leo’s projected margin favors when he is 1 point or more off the line, and only when his margin and his win chance agree on the winner.
  • Total: over or under when Leo’s total is 1.5 to 7 points off the line.
  • Prop calls: Leo’s side of the posted line for quarterback passing, running back rushing and receiver yards. Inside 5% of the line there is no call.
  • Touchdown chances have no posted line, so there is no side. They are graded as chances: of the players Leo gave about a 25% chance, roughly 1 in 4 should score.
  • Under 50 graded calls a record is an early read. Losses stay on the record. Players ruled out are marked excluded, never deleted.
Earlier rules: the record before the three calls

From November 2 every game gets a moneyline, spread and total call or a PASS. These are the records under the rules that came before; they stay on the record as history.

Why Leo misses, and what it is testing

What Leo is learning

Every missed game gets one reason, worked out from the data when the game is graded. Across 96 missed games this season the most common is Variance (44). Misses that were Leo’s own error: 2 in Week 10, 1 in Week 9. Only misses that are Leo’s own error point to a model change, and changes are made one at a time, each backed by a backtest.

Changes being tested

  • Quarterback passing touchdowns testing The first version missed its bar in the 2025 season, so it is not on the site. A second version is set up with the same bar.
  • Receiver and running back unders tracking Graded from November 1. Judged at 50 calls on results and on whether the line moved toward Leo.
  • Red zone touches in the touchdown model rejected No gain on the 2023 to 2025 seasons; touches and touchdown history already carry it.
  • Taking the spread from the win chance model rejected Lost more than the current spread model on the 2023 to 2025 seasons.
  • Running back calibration rejected No variant moved the average miss by more than 0.3 yards, so the setting stays.
